# 📸 Hawaiʻi 2026: photo guide

> Every hotel, restaurant, bar and place named anywhere in the plan, with up to six photos each, so you can see what you're picking before you book.
> **104 places · 543 photos** · Oʻahu, Maui, and Kauaʻi (the backup island). Built 2026-10-18.
> Each place also has a **🔎 Google Images** link (for more photos) and a **📍 Google Maps** link. Places in the current plan are marked **✅ in the plan**.
> Photos are linked, not copied. Wikimedia Commons photos are credited with their author and license; hotel and restaurant photos come from the official site or the Expedia listing and are credited by source.
> Plan: [`tentative_plan.md`](tentative_plan.md) · island guides: [`oahu.md`](oahu.md), [`maui.md`](maui.md) · hotels: [`hotels.md`](hotels.md) · birthday: [`birthday.md`](birthday.md)

<a id="photo-credits-and-notes"></a>

## Photo credits and notes

- **Wikimedia Commons** photos are free-licensed (CC BY, CC BY-SA, CC0 or public domain). Each one links to its Commons page, where the full author and license details are.
- **Hotel and restaurant** photos are the businesses' own marketing photos, linked from their official site or Expedia listing for personal trip planning. Click a photo to open its source.
- Some photos are from before the August 2023 Maui fires. Lahaina town is closed and rebuilding; Kāʻanapali and Kapalua are open.
- Linked images can break if the source site moves them. If one disappears, use the place's 🔎 Google Images link.
- Photos were checked by eye for the right place. Several automatic matches were dropped, such as a Lake Tahoe "Secret Cove" and Idaho's "Twin Falls".
